Base: current `jetson/c1` HEAD. Allowed: `bin/lab`, `lab/test_lab_hosts.py`. Do not touch `lab/jobs/*`, `lab/export/*`, `lab/images/*` (live evidence). No docker/GPU/`lab run`.
Fix, with a test each:
1. `lab run` must NOT re-evaluate admission for jobs lacking `admission.json` (pre-J-T4 x86 jobs): treat as `legacy-unrecorded`, run as before. Accept docker memory suffixes `b,k,m,g` with optional `b` (`16gb`, `512mb`).

Exceptions inside admission → decision `needs_information` with the error text, never a traceback/half-written job.

#!/usr/bin/env python3
"""lab — feasibility core of a "lab": image(container) + tests -> job -> attempts -> evidence. No UI. Discovery prototype.
  lab image add NAME REF [--platform P] [--requirements BUILD.json]   register an image by immutable id/digest
  lab submit --image N --model P --tests a,b --key K [--res cpus=8,memory=16g,timeout_s=300]   idempotent by key; job.json is immutable
  lab run JOB                       new attempt (retry = new attempt); no GPU admission control (lock removed)
  lab cancel JOB ATT                request cancellation (confirmed only when owned containers are gone)
  lab status [JOB]                  states
  lab reconcile                     inspect non-terminal attempts against REAL processes/containers; never trusts heartbeats
  lab register JOB ATT              register result into index (idempotent; stale/conflicting rejected)
Store: $LAB/{images,jobs,index}. Fault injection for recovery tests: LAB_FAULT=die_after_start|die_in_export
"""
import json, sys, os, re, time, uuid, hashlib, fcntl, subprocess, argparse, shutil, datetime as dt, glob, shlex, importlib.util, math
LAB = os.environ.get("LAB", os.path.expanduser("~/projects/siminfeng/campaign/lab"))
CID = os.environ.get("CAMPAIGN_ID", "c1")
now = lambda: dt.datetime.now(dt.timezone.utc).isoformat()
def jd(o): return json.dumps(o, sort_keys=True)
def sha(p):
    h = hashlib.sha256()
    with open(p, "rb") as f:
        for b in iter(lambda: f.read(1 << 20), b""): h.update(b)
    return h.hexdigest()
def sha_s(s): return hashlib.sha256(s.encode()).hexdigest()
ROOT = os.path.dirname(os.path.dirname(os.path.realpath(__file__)))
def docker_argv(cmd):
    prefix = os.environ.get("DOCKER_CMD_PREFIX", "")
    return shlex.split(prefix) + [shlex.join(cmd)] if prefix and cmd[0] == "docker" else cmd

def sh(cmd, **kw): return subprocess.run(docker_argv(cmd), capture_output=True, text=True, **kw)

def load_host(host_id=None):
    if host_id is None:
        try: host_id = open(os.path.expanduser("~/.config/siminfeng/host_id")).read().strip()
        except FileNotFoundError: host_id = "x86-main"
        host_id = host_id or "x86-main"
    if not re.fullmatch(r"[A-Za-z0-9_-]+", host_id): raise ValueError("invalid host id")
    path = os.path.join(ROOT, "lab", "hosts", host_id + ".json")
    profile = json.load(open(path))
    if profile["host_id"] != host_id: raise ValueError("host profile id mismatch")
    return profile, sha(path)

def gpu_request(host):
    if host["gpu"]["request"] == "gpus-all": return ["--gpus", "all"]
    if host["gpu"]["request"] == "nvidia-runtime-env":
        return ["--runtime", "nvidia", "-e", "NVIDIA_VISIBLE_DEVICES=all", "-e", "NVIDIA_DRIVER_CAPABILITIES=compute,utility"]
    raise ValueError("unsupported GPU request")

def memory_bytes(value):
    m = re.fullmatch(r"(\d+(?:\.\d+)?)\s*([bkmg]?)", str(value).lower())
    if not m: raise ValueError("invalid memory: " + str(value))
    return float(m[1]) * 1024 ** ("bkmg".index(m[2]) if m[2] else 0)

def admission(img, spec, host, profile_sha):
    rejected, missing, notes = [], [], []
    req = img.get("target_requirements") or {}
    legacy = host["host_id"] == "x86-main" and not img.get("platform") and not req
    if legacy: notes.append("legacy image record: platform unverified")
    else:
        if not img.get("platform"): missing.append("image platform missing")
        if not req: missing.append("image target_requirements missing")
        for source in (img, req):
            if source.get("platform") and source["platform"] != host["platform"]: rejected.append("platform mismatch: " + str(source["platform"]))
        for key, expected in (("cuda_arch", host["gpu"]["arch"]), ("l4t_major", host.get("l4t_major"))):
            if key in req and str(req[key]) != str(expected): rejected.append(key + " mismatch")
        if req:
            for key in ("platform", "cuda_arch", "runtime") + (("l4t_major",) if host.get("l4t_major") else ()):
                if not req.get(key): missing.append("image requirement missing: " + key)
    for key in ("cpus", "memory"):
        try:
            convert = float if key == "cpus" else memory_bytes
            value = convert(spec["resources"][key]); limit = convert(host["resources_max"][key])
            if not math.isfinite(value) or value <= 0 or value > limit: rejected.append(key + " exceeds host max or is invalid")
        except (ValueError, TypeError): rejected.append("invalid resource: " + key)
    for test in spec["tests"]:
        td = TESTS.get(test, {})
        if td.get("memory") and memory_bytes(td["memory"]) > memory_bytes(host["resources_max"]["memory"]): rejected.append("test memory exceeds host max: " + test)
        if td.get("needs_data") and not spec.get("data"): missing.append("data path missing")
        if td.get("model_file") and not os.path.isfile(os.path.join(os.path.dirname(spec["model"]), td["model_file"])): missing.append("test model path missing: " + test)
    for key in ("model", "data"):
        if (key == "model" or spec.get(key)) and not os.path.isfile(spec.get(key) or ""): missing.append(key + " path missing")
    return dict(decision="rejected" if rejected else "needs_information" if missing else "accepted", reasons=rejected + missing + notes,
                host_id=host["host_id"], host_profile_sha256=profile_sha)

def require_admission(*decisions):
    for decision in decisions:
        if decision and decision["decision"] != "accepted":
            print("REJECT: " + decision["decision"] + ": " + "; ".join(decision["reasons"])); sys.exit(10)

_observer = None
def observer():
    global _observer
    if _observer is None:
        spec = importlib.util.spec_from_file_location("lab_resource_observe", os.path.join(ROOT, "cc/resource_observe/resource_observe.py"))
        _observer = importlib.util.module_from_spec(spec); spec.loader.exec_module(_observer)
    return _observer

def parse_memory(text, backend):
    if backend == "tegrastats":
        metrics, unavailable, errors = observer().parse_tegrastats(text)
        value = next((m["value"] for m in metrics if m["name"] == "ram_used"), None)
        return value, None if value is not None else "tegrastats RAM field missing or unparseable"
    text = text.strip()
    if re.fullmatch(r"\d+", text): return int(text), None
    return None, "nvidia-smi memory.used unavailable or unparseable: " + repr(text)

class MemorySampler:
    def __init__(self, host):
        self.backend = host["gpu"]["sampler"]; self.reader = None; self.reason = None
        if self.backend == "tegrastats":
            try: self.reader = observer().TegrastatsReader(250)
            except OSError as ex: self.reason = str(ex)
    def sample(self):
        if self.backend == "tegrastats":
            lines = self.reader.drain() if self.reader else []
            return parse_memory(lines[-1], self.backend) if lines else (None, self.reason or "no tegrastats sample available")
        try:
            r = sh(["nvidia-smi","--query-gpu=memory.used","--format=csv,noheader,nounits"])
            return parse_memory(r.stdout, self.backend) if not r.returncode else (None, "nvidia-smi failed: " + r.stderr.strip())
        except OSError as ex: return None, str(ex)
    def close(self):
        if self.reader:
            self.reader.close()
            try: self.reader.p.wait(timeout=2)
            except subprocess.TimeoutExpired: self.reader.p.kill(); self.reader.p.wait()

def append(path, rec):
    os.makedirs(os.path.dirname(path), exist_ok=True)
    fd = os.open(path, os.O_WRONLY | os.O_APPEND | os.O_CREAT, 0o644)
    try: fcntl.flock(fd, fcntl.LOCK_EX); os.write(fd, (jd(rec) + "\n").encode()); os.fsync(fd)
    finally: os.close(fd)
def proc_start(pid):
    try: return open(f"/proc/{pid}/stat").read().rsplit(")", 1)[1].split()[19]
    except Exception: return None
def last_state(adir):
    p = os.path.join(adir, "state.jsonl")
    rows = [json.loads(l) for l in open(p)] if os.path.exists(p) else []
    return rows[-1] if rows else None

# ---- test definitions: how an image's tools are exercised and judged (the "tests" half of a lab) ----
def load_tests():
    t = {}
    import glob as g
    for f in sorted(g.glob(os.path.join(LAB, "tests", "*.json"))): t.update(json.load(open(f)))
    return t
TESTS = load_tests()
def assess(test, out, err, rc):
    txt = out + "\n" + err
    if TESTS[test].get("parser") == "ppl":
        m = re.search(r"Final estimate: PPL = ([\d.]+) \+/- ([\d.]+)", txt)
        return dict(ok=rc == 0 and bool(m), ppl=float(m.group(1)) if m else None, ppl_stderr=float(m.group(2)) if m else None)
    if TESTS[test].get("parser") == "kl":
        g = lambda pat: (lambda m: float(m.group(1)) if m else None)(re.search(pat, txt))
        return dict(ok=rc == 0 and g(r"Mean\s+KLD:\s+([\d.]+)") is not None, kld=g(r"Mean\s+KLD:\s+([\d.]+)"), kld_p99=g(r"99.0%\s+KLD:\s+([\d.]+)"),
                    top1_pct=g(r"Same top p:\s+([\d.]+)"), ppl_ratio=g(r"Mean PPL\(Q\)/PPL\(base\)\s*:\s+([\d.]+)"), ppl=g(r"Mean PPL\(Q\)\s*:\s+([\d.]+)"))
    if TESTS[test].get("parser") == "completion":
        m = re.findall(r"\beval time =\s+([\d.]+) ms /\s+(\d+) (?:runs|tokens).*?([\d.]+) tokens per second", txt)
        pe = re.findall(r"prompt eval time =\s+([\d.]+) ms /\s+(\d+) tokens.*?([\d.]+) tokens per second", txt)
        dec = [x for x in m]   # lines starting with 'eval time' (decode); 'prompt eval time' captured separately
        return dict(ok=rc == 0 and bool(dec), decode_tps=float(dec[-1][2]) if dec else None, decode_tokens=int(dec[-1][1]) if dec else None,
                    prefill_tps=float(pe[-1][2]) if pe else None, prefill_tokens=int(pe[-1][1]) if pe else None, text_head=out.strip()[-160:])
    if TESTS[test].get("parser") == "gen":
        return dict(ok=rc == 0 and len(out.strip()) > 20, chars=len(out.strip()), head=out.strip()[:120])
    if TESTS[test].get("parser") == "bench_json":
        try: rows = json.loads(out[out.index("["):out.rindex("]") + 1])
        except Exception: return dict(ok=False, parse_error=True)
        return dict(ok=rc == 0 and bool(rows), samples=[dict(n_prompt=r["n_prompt"], n_gen=r["n_gen"], avg_ts=r["avg_ts"], samples_ts=r.get("samples_ts")) for r in rows])
    return dict(ok=rc == 0)

def gpu_state(host=None):
    host = host or load_host()[0]
    if host["gpu"]["sampler"] == "tegrastats":
        return dict(gpu=None, compute_apps=None, unavailable=["GPU memory and compute-app enumeration unavailable on shared DRAM; tegrastats RAM is system-shared-dram"])
    try:
        r = sh(["nvidia-smi","--query-gpu=memory.used,clocks.sm,temperature.gpu,power.draw","--format=csv,noheader"])
        a = sh(["nvidia-smi","--query-compute-apps=pid,used_memory","--format=csv,noheader"])
        q = r.stdout.strip(); apps = a.stdout.strip().splitlines()
        if r.returncode or a.returncode or not q or re.search(r"N/A|Not Supported", q + a.stdout, re.I):
            return dict(gpu=None, compute_apps=None, unavailable=["nvidia-smi fields unavailable: " + (r.stderr + a.stderr + q).strip()])
        return dict(gpu=q, compute_apps=apps)
    except OSError as ex: return dict(gpu=None, compute_apps=None, unavailable=[str(ex)])

def read_int(path):
    try: return int(open(path).read().strip())
    except Exception: return None

def cid_cgroup(cname):
    cid = sh(["docker", "inspect", "-f", "{{.Id}}", cname]).stdout.strip()
    return f"/sys/fs/cgroup/system.slice/docker-{cid}.scope" if cid else None

def cmd_image(a):
    ref = a.ref; r = sh(["docker", "image", "inspect", ref, "-f", "{{.Id}}|{{json .RepoDigests}}|{{.Created}}"])
    if r.returncode: sys.exit("image not present locally: " + r.stderr)
    iid, rd, created = r.stdout.strip().split("|")
    rec = dict(name=a.name, ref=ref, image_id=iid, repo_digests=json.loads(rd), created=created, registered=now())
    requirements = getattr(a, "requirements", None)
    if requirements:
        build = json.load(open(requirements)); rec["target_requirements"] = build.get("target_requirements", {})
        rec["platform"] = build.get("platform") or rec["target_requirements"].get("platform")
    if getattr(a, "platform", None): rec["platform"] = a.platform
    p = os.path.join(LAB, "images", a.name + ".json"); os.makedirs(os.path.dirname(p), exist_ok=True)
    if os.path.exists(p) and json.load(open(p))["image_id"] != iid: sys.exit("name already bound to a different image id (images are immutable)")
    json.dump(rec, open(p, "w"), indent=1); print(jd(rec))

def archive_ids(path):
    """Identities read from the docker-save archive itself: config digest (image config) and top-level index digest (what the containerd store reports as Id)."""
    import tarfile
    out = dict(config=None, index=None)
    with tarfile.open(path) as t:
        names = t.getnames()
        if "manifest.json" in names:
            m = json.load(t.extractfile("manifest.json"))
            if m and m[0].get("Config", "").startswith("blobs/sha256/"): out["config"] = "sha256:" + m[0]["Config"].split("/")[-1]
        if "index.json" in names:
            ms = json.load(t.extractfile("index.json")).get("manifests", [])
            out["index_entries"] = [m.get("digest") for m in ms]
            if len(ms) == 1: out["index"] = ms[0].get("digest")
    return out

def cmd_imageload(a):
    """Admit an engine image delivered as a docker archive. Order: (1) archive bytes hash, (2) archive's own config digest vs expected,
    (3) docker load, (4) loaded image id must equal the archive's index digest (the containerd store's notion of Id)."""
    got = sha(a.archive)
    if got != a.sha256: print(f"REJECT: archive sha256 mismatch (expected {a.sha256[:12]}..., got {got[:12]}...)"); sys.exit(10)
    ids = archive_ids(a.archive)
    if ids["config"] != a.expect_config_id: print(f"REJECT: archive config digest {str(ids['config'])[:19]}... != expected {a.expect_config_id[:19]}..."); sys.exit(12)
    r = sh(["docker", "load", "-i", a.archive])
    if r.returncode: print("REJECT: docker load failed:", r.stderr[-300:]); sys.exit(11)
    cands = ids.get("index_entries") or [ids["index"] or ids["config"]]
    want = next((c for c in cands if sh(["docker", "image", "inspect", c, "-f", "{{.Id}}"]).stdout.strip() == c), None)
    if want is None: print(f"REJECT: no loaded image has an id among the archive's index entries {[c[:19] for c in cands]}"); sys.exit(13)
    a.ref = want; a.op = "add"; cmd_image(a); print("admitted:", a.name, "config", ids["config"][:19], "docker-id", want[:19])

def cmd_chaos(a):
    ow = json.load(open(os.path.join(LAB, "jobs", a.job, a.att, "owner.json"))); cs = owned_containers(a.att, ow["token"])
    for c in cs: sh(["docker", "kill", c])
    append(os.path.join(LAB, "jobs", a.job, a.att, "chaos.jsonl"), dict(ts=now(), action="kill-sandbox", containers=cs)); print("killed owned containers:", cs)

def cmd_submit(a):
    img = json.load(open(os.path.join(LAB, "images", a.image + ".json")))
    host, profile_sha = load_host()
    res = dict(cpus=str(host["resources_max"]["cpus"]), memory=host["resources_max"]["memory"], pids="512", timeout_s="300", gpu_wait_s="600"); res.update(dict(kv.split("=") for kv in (a.res or "").split(",") if kv))
    spec = dict(image=a.image, image_id=img["image_id"], model=os.path.abspath(a.model), model_sha256=a.model_sha256 or (sha(a.model) if os.path.isfile(a.model) else None),
                data=a.data, data_sha256=(sha(a.data) if a.data and os.path.isfile(a.data) else None), tests=a.tests.split(","), resources=res, key=a.key)
    jid = "job-" + sha_s(a.key)[:10]; jdir = os.path.join(LAB, "jobs", jid); p = os.path.join(jdir, "job.json")
    if os.path.exists(p):
        old = json.load(open(p))["spec"]
        if old != spec: sys.exit(f"idempotency key reused with DIFFERENT spec -> rejected (job {jid})")
        print(f"duplicate submission: existing {jid}"); return
    os.makedirs(jdir); json.dump(dict(job=jid, submitted=now(), spec=spec, spec_sha256=sha_s(jd(spec))), open(p, "w"), indent=1); os.chmod(p, 0o444)
    decision = admission(img, spec, host, profile_sha)
    apath = os.path.join(jdir, "admission.json")
    json.dump(decision, open(apath, "w"), indent=1); os.chmod(apath, 0o444)
    append(os.path.join(jdir, "job.log"), dict(ts=now(), ev="submitted")); print(jid)

def owned_containers(att, token=None):
    r = sh(["docker", "ps", "-aq", "--filter", f"label=lab_attempt={att}"]).stdout.split(); out = []
    for c in r:
        lab = json.loads(sh(["docker", "inspect", c, "-f", "{{json .Config.Labels}}"]).stdout)
        if token is None or lab.get("lab_token") == token: out.append(c)
    return out


def container_argv(host, job, att, token, cname, spec, img, td):
    res = spec["resources"]
    cmd = ["docker","run","--rm","--name",cname,"--label",f"campaign={CID}","--label",f"lab_job={job}","--label",f"lab_attempt={att}","--label",f"lab_token={token}",
           *sum([["-e", f"{k}={v}"] for k, v in td.get("env", {}).items()], []),*gpu_request(host),"--cpus",res["cpus"],"--memory",td.get("memory", res["memory"]),"--memory-swap",td.get("memory", res["memory"]),"--pids-limit",res["pids"],"-v",f"{os.path.dirname(spec['model'])}:/m:ro",*td.get("docker_args", [])]
    if spec.get("data"): cmd += ["-v", f"{os.path.dirname(spec['data'])}:/d:ro"]
    mfile = td.get("model_file", os.path.basename(spec["model"]))
    cmd += ["--entrypoint","/app/"+td["tool"], img["image_id"], "-m", "/m/"+mfile]
    if td.get("needs_data"): cmd += ["-f", "/d/"+os.path.basename(spec["data"])]
    cmd += td["args"]
    return cmd

def cmd_run(a):
    job = json.load(open(os.path.join(LAB, "jobs", a.job, "job.json"))); spec = job["spec"]; jdir = os.path.join(LAB, "jobs", a.job)
    img = json.load(open(os.path.join(LAB, "images", spec["image"] + ".json")))
    host, profile_sha = load_host()
    apath = os.path.join(jdir, "admission.json")
    recorded = json.load(open(apath)) if os.path.exists(apath) else None
    current = admission(img, spec, host, profile_sha)
    require_admission(recorded, current)
    for prev in sorted(glob.glob(os.path.join(jdir, "att-*"))):   # duplicate-delivery / replacement guard
        ls = last_state(prev); pa = os.path.basename(prev)
        if ls and ls["state"] != "terminal":
            ow = json.load(open(os.path.join(prev, "owner.json")))
            if proc_start(ow["runner_pid"]) == ow["runner_start"] or owned_containers(pa, ow["token"]):
                print(f"REJECT: {pa} is still active (runner alive or owned containers present); no duplicate execution"); sys.exit(8)
            print(f"REJECT: {pa} is non-terminal and its owner is gone; run `lab reconcile` to confirm it stopped before replacement"); sys.exit(9)
    n = len(glob.glob(os.path.join(jdir, "att-*"))) + 1; att = f"att-{n}"; adir = os.path.join(jdir, att); os.makedirs(adir)
    token = uuid.uuid4().hex
    json.dump(dict(attempt=att, runner_pid=os.getpid(), runner_start=proc_start(os.getpid()), token=token, started=now()), open(os.path.join(adir, "owner.json"), "w"))
    S = lambda s, **kw: append(os.path.join(adir, "state.jsonl"), dict(ts=now(), job=a.job, attempt=att, state=s, **kw))
    S("queued"); res = spec["resources"]
    S("preparing")
    if sha(spec["model"]) != spec["model_sha256"]: S("terminal", execution="not_started", reason="model hash mismatch"); sys.exit(4)
    S("running", admission="none (gpu lock removed by user decision)", gpu_pre=gpu_state(host))
    results = {}; cancelled = False; interrupted = False; sandbox_died = False
    for ti, tfull in enumerate(spec["tests"]):
        t = tfull; td = TESTS[t]; cname = f"lab-{CID}-{a.job}-{att}-{t}-{ti}".lower()
        cmd = container_argv(host, a.job, att, token, cname, spec, img, td)
        mfile = td.get("model_file", os.path.basename(spec["model"]))
        if td.get("cold_cache"):   # evict ONLY this model file's cached pages (posix_fadvise); never a host-wide flush
            fd = os.open(os.path.join(os.path.dirname(spec["model"]), mfile), os.O_RDONLY); os.posix_fadvise(fd, 0, 0, os.POSIX_FADV_DONTNEED); os.close(fd)
        o, e = os.path.join(adir, f"{t}#{ti}.stdout"), os.path.join(adir, f"{t}#{ti}.stderr"); ts = time.monotonic()
        with open(o,"w") as fo, open(e,"w") as fe:
            p = subprocess.Popen(docker_argv(cmd), stdout=fo, stderr=fe, start_new_session=True)
            S("running", test=t, container=cname)
            if os.environ.get("LAB_FAULT") == "die_after_start": time.sleep(1.5); os.kill(os.getpid(), 9)
            sampler = MemorySampler(host)
            try:
                base_gpu, sample_reason = sampler.sample()
                peak_gpu, peak_host, io_rbytes, cg = None, 0, 0, None
                nsamp = 0; tlast = 0
                while p.poll() is None:
                    if time.monotonic() - tlast > 0.25:   # memory sampler: GPU total used (device is otherwise idle) + container cgroup
                        tlast = time.monotonic(); g, why = sampler.sample()
                        if why: sample_reason = why
                        if g is not None: peak_gpu = max(peak_gpu, g) if peak_gpu is not None else g; nsamp += 1
                        cg = cg or cid_cgroup(cname)
                        if cg:
                            v = read_int(cg + "/memory.peak") or read_int(cg + "/memory.current"); peak_host = max(peak_host, v or 0)
                            try: io_rbytes = max(io_rbytes, sum(int(x.split("=")[1]) for l in open(cg + "/io.stat") for x in l.split() if x.startswith("rbytes=")))
                            except Exception: pass
                    if os.path.exists(os.path.join(adir, "cancel_requested")): cancelled = True; sh(["docker","kill",cname]); break
                    if time.monotonic() - ts > float(res["timeout_s"]): sh(["docker","kill",cname]); interrupted = True; break
                    time.sleep(0.3)
                rc = p.wait()
            finally:
                sampler.close()
        died = (not cancelled) and (not interrupted) and rc in (137, 143)   # SIGKILL/SIGTERM not requested by us: sandbox/container terminated externally (or OOM)
        results[f"{t}#{ti}"] = dict(exit_code=rc, elapsed_s=round(time.monotonic()-ts,3), partial=died, mem=dict(gpu_peak_mib=peak_gpu if sampler.backend == "nvml" else None, gpu_baseline_mib=base_gpu if sampler.backend == "nvml" else None, gpu_delta_mib=(peak_gpu-base_gpu if sampler.backend == "nvml" and peak_gpu is not None and base_gpu is not None else None), host_cgroup_peak_mib=round(peak_host/2**20,1), io_read_mib=round(io_rbytes/2**20,1), samples=nsamp, method="nvidia-smi memory.used polled 4Hz (device otherwise idle; includes CUDA context) + cgroup memory.peak"), **assess(t, open(o, errors="replace").read(), open(e, errors="replace").read(), rc))
        mem = results[f"{t}#{ti}"]["mem"]
        if sampler.backend == "tegrastats":
            mem.update(shared_dram_peak_mib=peak_gpu, shared_dram_baseline_mib=base_gpu, scope="system-shared-dram", method="tegrastats RAM used polled 4Hz + cgroup memory.peak", gpu_memory_reason="shared DRAM is not GPU memory")
        if sample_reason or peak_gpu is None:
            mem["unavailable"] = sample_reason or "no memory samples"
        if died: sandbox_died = True; S("sandbox_terminated", test=t, exit_code=rc, last_durable_bytes=os.path.getsize(o), last_durable_mtime=dt.datetime.fromtimestamp(os.path.getmtime(o), dt.timezone.utc).isoformat())
        if cancelled or interrupted or died: break
    post = gpu_state(host)
    S("assessing")
    left = owned_containers(att, token)
    if cancelled: S("cancel_confirmed" if not left else "cancel_unconfirmed", owned_left=left)
    files = {f: sha(os.path.join(adir, f)) for f in sorted(os.listdir(adir)) if f.endswith((".stdout",".stderr"))}
    result = dict(job=a.job, attempt=att, image_id=spec["image_id"], token_sha=sha_s(token),
      execution=dict(status="cancelled" if cancelled else "interrupted_sandbox_terminated" if sandbox_died else "timeout_killed" if interrupted else "completed" if all(r["exit_code"]==0 for r in results.values()) else "failed"),
      quality=dict(tests=results, all_ok=(not cancelled and not interrupted and not sandbox_died and len(results)==len(spec["tests"]) and all(r["ok"] for r in results.values()))),
      performance=dict(gpu_post=post, exclusive_before_after=(post["compute_apps"] == []), note="exclusivity = no compute apps visible at end; during-run interference not observed by lab"),
      evidence=dict(files=files, coverage=dict(tests_expected=len(spec["tests"]), tests_started=len(results), tests_completed_cleanly=sum(1 for r in results.values() if not r.get("partial") and r["exit_code"]==0), interrupted=sandbox_died or interrupted or cancelled, note="incomplete data is never scored; affected comparisons invalid")), cleanup=dict(owned_containers_left=left, confirmed=not left))
    json.dump(result, open(os.path.join(adir,"result.json"),"w"), indent=1)
    S("exporting"); export(a.job, att, adir)
    S("terminal", result_sha256=sha(os.path.join(adir,"result.json"))); print(jd(result)[:700])

def export(job, att, adir):
    """stage -> fsync -> rename; staged copy is retained until the durable copy verifies."""
    dest = os.path.join(LAB, "export", job, att); stage = dest + ".staging"
    shutil.rmtree(stage, ignore_errors=True); os.makedirs(os.path.dirname(dest), exist_ok=True); shutil.copytree(adir, stage)
    if os.environ.get("LAB_FAULT") == "die_in_export": os.kill(os.getpid(), 9)
    manifest = {f: sha(os.path.join(stage, f)) for f in sorted(os.listdir(stage))}
    json.dump(manifest, open(os.path.join(stage, "MANIFEST.json"), "w"), indent=1)
    shutil.rmtree(dest, ignore_errors=True); os.rename(stage, dest)
    ok = all(sha(os.path.join(dest, f)) == h for f, h in manifest.items())
    if not ok: raise SystemExit("export verify failed; staging lost?")

def cmd_cancel(a):
    adir = os.path.join(LAB, "jobs", a.job, a.att); open(os.path.join(adir, "cancel_requested"), "w").write(now())
    append(os.path.join(adir, "state.jsonl"), dict(ts=now(), attempt=a.att, state="cancel_requested")); print("cancel requested (NOT confirmed)")

def cmd_status(a):
    for path in sorted(glob.glob(os.path.join(LAB, "jobs", a.job or "*", "job.json"))):
        apath = os.path.join(os.path.dirname(path), "admission.json")
        decision = json.load(open(apath)) if os.path.exists(apath) else {"decision": "legacy (unrecorded)"}
        print(os.path.basename(os.path.dirname(path)), "admission=" + decision["decision"], "; ".join(decision.get("reasons", [])))
    for adir in sorted(glob.glob(os.path.join(LAB, "jobs", a.job or "*", "att-*"))):
        s = last_state(adir); print(adir.split("jobs/")[1], s["state"] if s else None, {k: v for k, v in (s or {}).items() if k in ("execution","reason","test")})

def cmd_reconcile(a):
    for adir in sorted(glob.glob(os.path.join(LAB, "jobs", "*", "att-*"))):
        job, att = adir.split("jobs/")[1].split("/"); s = last_state(adir); ow = json.load(open(os.path.join(adir, "owner.json")))
        stage = os.path.join(LAB, "export", job, att + ".staging")
        if s and s["state"] == "terminal" and not os.path.exists(stage): continue
        alive = proc_start(ow["runner_pid"]) == ow["runner_start"]
        owned = owned_containers(att, ow["token"]); S = lambda st, **kw: append(os.path.join(adir, "state.jsonl"), dict(ts=now(), job=job, attempt=att, state=st, reconciler=True, **kw))
        if alive: print(job, att, "runner ALIVE (pid %d) -> leave alone; state=%s" % (ow["runner_pid"], s["state"])); continue
        if s["state"] == "terminal" and os.path.exists(stage):   # died mid-export after terminal? finish export from retained staging
            print(job, att, "staged export retained -> completing"); export(job, att, adir); continue
        print(job, att, f"runner DEAD; last={s['state']}; owned containers still present: {owned}")
        for c in owned: sh(["docker", "kill", c]); sh(["docker", "rm", "-f", c])
        left = owned_containers(att, ow["token"])
        if os.path.exists(os.path.join(adir, "result.json")):   # result was already produced: NEVER overwrite; just finish durable export
            print(job, att, "result.json already exists -> resuming export only (no rescoring, no overwrite)")
            S("exporting", resumed=True); export(job, att, adir)
            S("terminal", execution="completed_before_runner_death", result_sha256=sha(os.path.join(adir, "result.json")), killed=owned); continue
        partial = sorted(f for f in os.listdir(adir) if f.endswith((".stdout", ".stderr")))
        res = dict(job=job, attempt=att, execution=dict(status="interrupted"), quality=dict(assessed=False, note="incomplete data is never scored"),
                   evidence=dict(partial_files=partial), cleanup=dict(owned_containers_left=left, confirmed=not left))
        json.dump(res, open(os.path.join(adir, "result.json"), "w"), indent=1); S("exporting")
        if os.environ.get("LAB_FAULT") != "die_in_export": export(job, att, adir)
        S("terminal", execution="interrupted", result_sha256=sha(os.path.join(adir, "result.json")), killed=owned)

def cmd_register(a):
    adir = os.path.join(LAB, "jobs", a.job, a.att); res = json.load(open(os.path.join(adir, "result.json"))); ow = json.load(open(os.path.join(adir, "owner.json")))
    idx = os.path.join(LAB, "index", "results.jsonl"); os.makedirs(os.path.dirname(idx), exist_ok=True)
    key = (a.job, a.att); rsha = sha(os.path.join(adir, "result.json")); rows = [json.loads(l) for l in open(idx)] if os.path.exists(idx) else []
    if res.get("token_sha") and res["token_sha"] != sha_s(ow["token"]): print("REJECT: stale/foreign attempt token"); sys.exit(5)
    for r in rows:
        if (r["job"], r["attempt"]) == key:
            if r["result_sha256"] == rsha: print("already registered (identical) -> no-op"); return
            print("REJECT: conflicting result for an accepted attempt"); sys.exit(6)
    if not os.path.exists(os.path.join(LAB, "export", a.job, a.att, "MANIFEST.json")): print("REJECT: no confirmed durable export"); sys.exit(7)
    append(idx, dict(job=a.job, attempt=a.att, result_sha256=rsha, registered=now())); print("registered")

p = sp.add_parser("image"); p.add_argument("op"); p.add_argument("name"); p.add_argument("ref"); p.add_argument("--platform"); p.add_argument("--requirements"); p.set_defaults(f=cmd_image)
p = sp.add_parser("submit"); [p.add_argument("--" + x) for x in ("image","model","model-sha256","data","tests","key","res")]; p.set_defaults(f=cmd_submit)
p = sp.add_parser("image-load"); p.add_argument("name"); p.add_argument("--archive", required=True); p.add_argument("--sha256", required=True); p.add_argument("--expect-config-id", required=True); p.set_defaults(f=cmd_imageload)
p = sp.add_parser("chaos"); p.add_argument("job"); p.add_argument("att"); p.set_defaults(f=cmd_chaos)

"""Host contracts; all subprocess access is mocked, never execute a container/GPU."""
import hashlib
import importlib.machinery
import importlib.util
import json
from pathlib import Path
import shlex
from types import SimpleNamespace

import pytest

ROOT = Path(__file__).resolve().parents[1]


@pytest.fixture
def lab(monkeypatch, tmp_path):
    monkeypatch.setenv('LAB', str(tmp_path / 'lab'))
    monkeypatch.setenv('HOME', str(tmp_path / 'home'))
    monkeypatch.delenv('DOCKER_CMD_PREFIX', raising=False)
    loader = importlib.machinery.SourceFileLoader('lab_hosts_under_test', str(ROOT / 'bin/lab'))
    spec = importlib.util.spec_from_loader(loader.name, loader)
    mod = importlib.util.module_from_spec(spec)
    loader.exec_module(mod)
    def forbidden(*args, **kwargs):
        pytest.fail('unexpected subprocess invocation')
    monkeypatch.setattr(mod.subprocess, 'run', forbidden)
    monkeypatch.setattr(mod.subprocess, 'Popen', forbidden)
    return mod


def profile(lab, name='jetson-arm'):
    return lab.load_host(name)[0]


def test_profiles_and_default(lab, monkeypatch, tmp_path):
    host, digest = lab.load_host()
    assert host['host_id'] == 'x86-main'
    assert digest == hashlib.sha256((ROOT / 'lab/hosts/x86-main.json').read_bytes()).hexdigest()
    home = tmp_path / 'home/.config/siminfeng'
    home.mkdir(parents=True)
    (home / 'host_id').write_text('jetson-arm\n')
    host, digest = lab.load_host()
    assert host['platform'] == 'linux/arm64'
    assert host['resources_max'] == {'cpus': 6, 'memory': '5g'}
    assert host['l4t_major'] == 36
    (home / 'host_id').write_text('')
    assert lab.load_host()[0]['host_id'] == 'x86-main'
    with pytest.raises(ValueError): lab.load_host('../escape')
    with pytest.raises(FileNotFoundError): lab.load_host('unknown')


def test_x86_golden_argv(lab):
    # Literal golden list transcribed from bin/lab before J-T4.
    lab.CID = 'c1'
    spec = dict(model='/models/a.gguf', data='/data/p.txt', resources=dict(cpus='8', memory='16g', pids='512'))
    td = dict(tool='llama-perplexity', env={'A': 'two words'}, needs_data=True,
              memory='2g', model_file='other.gguf', docker_args=['--network', 'none'], args=['-c', '512'])
    golden = ['docker', 'run', '--rm', '--name', 'sandbox', '--label', 'campaign=c1', '--label',
              'lab_job=job-x', '--label', 'lab_attempt=att-1', '--label', 'lab_token=token',
              '-e', 'A=two words', '--gpus', 'all', '--cpus', '8', '--memory', '2g',
              '--memory-swap', '2g', '--pids-limit', '512', '-v', '/models:/m:ro', '--network', 'none',
              '-v', '/data:/d:ro', '--entrypoint', '/app/llama-perplexity', 'sha256:abc', '-m',
              '/m/other.gguf', '-f', '/d/p.txt', '-c', '512']
    args = ('job-x', 'att-1', 'token', 'sandbox', spec, {'image_id': 'sha256:abc'}, td)
    assert lab.container_argv(profile(lab, 'x86-main'), *args) == golden
    jetson = lab.container_argv(profile(lab), *args)
    at = golden.index('--gpus')
    assert jetson == golden[:at] + ['--runtime', 'nvidia', '-e', 'NVIDIA_VISIBLE_DEVICES=all',
                                   '-e', 'NVIDIA_DRIVER_CAPABILITIES=compute,utility'] + golden[at+2:]
    assert lab.docker_argv(golden) == golden


def test_prefix_is_one_shell_command(lab, monkeypatch):
    argv = ['docker', 'inspect', 'name with spaces;$(touch /tmp/no)', '-f', '{{.Id}}', "a'b"]
    monkeypatch.setenv('DOCKER_CMD_PREFIX', 'sg docker -c')
    wrapped = lab.docker_argv(argv)
    assert wrapped[:3] == ['sg', 'docker', '-c']
    assert len(wrapped) == 4
    assert shlex.split(wrapped[-1]) == argv
    seen = []
    monkeypatch.setattr(lab.subprocess, 'run', lambda cmd, **kw: seen.append(cmd))
    lab.sh(argv)
    assert seen == [wrapped]
    assert lab.docker_argv(['nvidia-smi']) == ['nvidia-smi']


@pytest.mark.parametrize('backend,text,value', [
    ('nvml', '123\n', 123), ('nvml', '0', 0), ('nvml', '[N/A]', None),
    ('nvml', '', None), ('nvml', '1\n2', None), ('nvml', 'nonsense', None),
    ('tegrastats', 'RAM 234/7620MB (lfb 1x4MB) CPU [0%@100]', 234),
    ('tegrastats', 'RAM 0/7620MB', 0), ('tegrastats', 'RAM [N/A]', None),
    ('tegrastats', 'truncated', None), ('tegrastats', '', None),
])
def test_parse_memory(lab, backend, text, value):
    got, reason = lab.parse_memory(text, backend)
    assert got == value
    assert bool(reason) == (value is None)


def test_sampler_nvml_failures(lab, monkeypatch):
    sampler = lab.MemorySampler(profile(lab, 'x86-main'))
    monkeypatch.setattr(lab.subprocess, 'run', lambda *a, **k: SimpleNamespace(stdout='[N/A]', stderr='', returncode=0))
    assert sampler.sample()[0] is None
    assert lab.gpu_state(profile(lab, 'x86-main'))['unavailable']
    monkeypatch.setattr(lab.subprocess, 'run', lambda *a, **k: SimpleNamespace(stdout='', stderr='denied', returncode=1))
    assert sampler.sample() == (None, 'nvidia-smi failed: denied')
    def absent(*a, **k): raise FileNotFoundError('missing tool')
    monkeypatch.setattr(lab.subprocess, 'run', absent)
    assert sampler.sample() == (None, 'missing tool')
    assert lab.gpu_state(profile(lab, 'x86-main'))['gpu'] is None
    sampler.close()


def test_tegrastats_sampler_and_state(lab, monkeypatch):
    calls = []
    class Child:
        stdout = iter([])
        def terminate(self): calls.append('terminate')
        def wait(self, **kwargs): calls.append('wait')
    def popen(cmd, **kwargs):
        calls.append(cmd)
        return Child()
    monkeypatch.setattr(lab.subprocess, 'Popen', popen)
    sampler = lab.MemorySampler(profile(lab))
    # Read directly from the queue to avoid depending on thread scheduling.
    sampler.reader.q.put('RAM 100/7620MB')
    value, reason = sampler.sample()
    assert value == 100 and reason is None
    assert sampler.sample()[0] is None
    sampler.close()
    assert calls == [['tegrastats', '--interval', '250'], 'terminate', 'wait']
    state = lab.gpu_state(profile(lab))
    assert state['gpu'] is None and state['compute_apps'] is None and state['unavailable']
    def absent(*a, **k): raise FileNotFoundError('tegrastats missing')
    monkeypatch.setattr(lab.subprocess, 'Popen', absent)
    sampler = lab.MemorySampler(profile(lab))
    assert sampler.sample() == (None, 'tegrastats missing')
    sampler.close()


@pytest.fixture
def admission_case(lab, tmp_path):
    model = tmp_path / 'a.gguf'; model.write_bytes(b'model')
    data = tmp_path / 'data.txt'; data.write_text('data')
    img = dict(image_id='sha256:abc', platform='linux/arm64', target_requirements={
        'platform': 'linux/arm64', 'cuda_arch': 'sm_87', 'l4t_major': 36,
        'runtime': 'nvidia (NVIDIA_VISIBLE_DEVICES required)'})
    spec = dict(model=str(model), data=str(data), tests=['smoke'], resources={'cpus': '6', 'memory': '5g'})
    return img, spec


@pytest.mark.parametrize('change,expected', [
    ('none', 'accepted'), ('platform', 'rejected'), ('req_platform', 'rejected'),
    ('cuda_arch', 'rejected'), ('l4t_major', 'rejected'), ('cpus', 'rejected'),
    ('memory', 'rejected'), ('bad_cpu', 'rejected'), ('bad_memory', 'rejected'),
    ('no_platform', 'needs_information'), ('no_requirements', 'needs_information'),
    ('no_runtime', 'needs_information'), ('model', 'needs_information'),
    ('data', 'needs_information'), ('needs_data', 'needs_information'),
    ('test_memory', 'rejected'), ('test_model', 'needs_information'),
    ('reject_over_missing', 'rejected'),
])
def test_admission_matrix(lab, admission_case, change, expected):
    img, spec = admission_case
    if change == 'platform': img['platform'] = 'linux/amd64'
    if change == 'req_platform': img['target_requirements']['platform'] = 'linux/amd64'
    if change in ('cuda_arch', 'l4t_major'): img['target_requirements'][change] = 'wrong'
    if change == 'cpus': spec['resources']['cpus'] = '7'
    if change == 'memory': spec['resources']['memory'] = '5121m'
    if change == 'bad_cpu': spec['resources']['cpus'] = 'nan'
    if change == 'bad_memory': spec['resources']['memory'] = 'oops'
    if change == 'no_platform': del img['platform']
    if change == 'no_requirements': del img['target_requirements']
    if change == 'no_runtime': del img['target_requirements']['runtime']
    if change in ('model', 'data'): spec[change] += '.missing'
    if change == 'needs_data':
        spec['data'] = None; lab.TESTS['smoke'] = {'needs_data': True}
    if change == 'test_memory': lab.TESTS['smoke'] = {'memory': '6g'}
    if change == 'test_model': lab.TESTS['smoke'] = {'model_file': 'missing.gguf'}
    if change == 'reject_over_missing':
        img['platform'] = 'linux/amd64'; spec['model'] += '.missing'
    decision = lab.admission(img, spec, profile(lab), 'digest')
    assert decision['decision'] == expected
    assert decision['host_profile_sha256'] == 'digest'
    assert decision['host_id'] == 'jetson-arm'
    assert bool(decision['reasons']) == (expected != 'accepted')


def test_x86_legacy(lab, admission_case):
    img, spec = admission_case
    spec['resources'] = {'cpus': '8', 'memory': '16g'}
    result = lab.admission({'image_id': 'legacy'}, spec, profile(lab, 'x86-main'), 'digest')
    assert result['decision'] == 'accepted'
    assert result['reasons'] == ['legacy image record: platform unverified']
    img['platform'] = 'linux/amd64'
    img['target_requirements'] = {'platform': 'linux/amd64', 'cuda_arch': 'sm_120', 'runtime': 'nvidia'}
    assert lab.admission(img, spec, profile(lab, 'x86-main'), 'digest')['decision'] == 'accepted'


@pytest.mark.parametrize('decision', ['accepted', 'rejected', 'needs_information'])
def test_submit_persists_readonly_idempotent(lab, monkeypatch, admission_case, decision, capsys):
    img, spec = admission_case
    host = profile(lab)
    monkeypatch.setattr(lab, 'load_host', lambda: (host, 'digest'))
    images = Path(lab.LAB) / 'images'; images.mkdir(parents=True)
    if decision == 'rejected': img['platform'] = 'linux/amd64'
    if decision == 'needs_information': spec['model'] += '.missing'
    (images / 'test.json').write_text(json.dumps(img))
    args = SimpleNamespace(image='test', model=spec['model'], model_sha256=None,
                           data=spec['data'], tests='smoke', key='key', res=None)
    lab.cmd_submit(args)
    jid = capsys.readouterr().out.strip()
    jobdir = Path(lab.LAB) / 'jobs' / jid
    saved = {}
    for name in ('job.json', 'admission.json'):
        path = jobdir / name
        assert path.stat().st_mode & 0o222 == 0
        saved[name] = path.read_bytes()
    assert json.loads(saved['admission.json'])['decision'] == decision
    assert json.loads(saved['job.json'])['spec']['resources']['memory'] == '5g'
    lab.cmd_submit(args)
    assert 'duplicate submission' in capsys.readouterr().out
    assert all((jobdir / name).read_bytes() == content for name, content in saved.items())
    args.res = 'cpus=1'
    with pytest.raises(SystemExit, match='DIFFERENT spec'): lab.cmd_submit(args)
    lab.cmd_status(SimpleNamespace(job=jid))
    assert 'admission=' + decision in capsys.readouterr().out


def test_image_requirements(lab, monkeypatch, tmp_path):
    build = json.loads((ROOT / 'cc/build_engine/results/jetson-c1/build-record.json').read_text())
    record = tmp_path / 'build.json'; record.write_text(json.dumps(build))
    monkeypatch.setenv('DOCKER_CMD_PREFIX', 'sg docker -c')
    seen = []
    def inspect(cmd, **kw):
        seen.append(cmd)
        return SimpleNamespace(returncode=0, stdout='sha256:abc|[]|today', stderr='')
    monkeypatch.setattr(lab.subprocess, 'run', inspect)
    args = SimpleNamespace(name='jetson', ref=build['tag'], requirements=str(record), platform=None)
    lab.cmd_image(args)
    result = json.loads((Path(lab.LAB) / 'images/jetson.json').read_text())
    assert result['platform'] == 'linux/arm64'
    assert result['target_requirements'] == build['target_requirements']
    assert shlex.split(seen[0][-1]) == ['docker', 'image', 'inspect', build['tag'], '-f', '{{.Id}}|{{json .RepoDigests}}|{{.Created}}']


@pytest.mark.parametrize('stored,current', [
    ('rejected', 'accepted'), ('needs_information', 'accepted'),
    ('accepted', 'rejected'), ('accepted', 'needs_information'),
    (None, 'rejected'),
])
def test_run_admission_guard_without_running_job(lab, stored, current, capsys):
    # Exercise the pre-attempt guard alone; never invoke lab run.
    make = lambda decision: {'decision': decision, 'reasons': ['guard reason']} if decision else None
    with pytest.raises(SystemExit) as ex:
        lab.require_admission(make(stored), make(current))
    assert ex.value.code == 10
    assert 'guard reason' in capsys.readouterr().out

    lab.require_admission(None, {'decision': 'accepted', 'reasons': []})
    lab.require_admission({'decision': 'accepted'}, {'decision': 'accepted'})

def export(job, att, adir):
    """stage -> fsync -> rename; staged copy is retained until the durable copy verifies."""
    dest = os.path.join(LAB, "export", job, att); stage = dest + ".staging"
    shutil.rmtree(stage, ignore_errors=True); os.makedirs(os.path.dirname(dest), exist_ok=True); shutil.copytree(adir, stage)
    if os.environ.get("LAB_FAULT") == "die_in_export": os.kill(os.getpid(), 9)
    manifest = {f: sha(os.path.join(stage, f)) for f in sorted(os.listdir(stage))}
    json.dump(manifest, open(os.path.join(stage, "MANIFEST.json"), "w"), indent=1)
    shutil.rmtree(dest, ignore_errors=True); os.rename(stage, dest)
    ok = all(sha(os.path.join(dest, f)) == h for f, h in manifest.items())
    if not ok: raise SystemExit("export verify failed; staging lost?")

def cmd_cancel(a):
    adir = os.path.join(LAB, "jobs", a.job, a.att); open(os.path.join(adir, "cancel_requested"), "w").write(now())
    append(os.path.join(adir, "state.jsonl"), dict(ts=now(), attempt=a.att, state="cancel_requested")); print("cancel requested (NOT confirmed)")

def cmd_status(a):
    for path in sorted(glob.glob(os.path.join(LAB, "jobs", a.job or "*", "job.json"))):
        apath = os.path.join(os.path.dirname(path), "admission.json")
        decision = json.load(open(apath)) if os.path.exists(apath) else {"decision": "legacy (unrecorded)"}
        print(os.path.basename(os.path.dirname(path)), "admission=" + decision["decision"], "; ".join(decision.get("reasons", [])))
    for adir in sorted(glob.glob(os.path.join(LAB, "jobs", a.job or "*", "att-*"))):
        s = last_state(adir); print(adir.split("jobs/")[1], s["state"] if s else None, {k: v for k, v in (s or {}).items() if k in ("execution","reason","test")})

def cmd_reconcile(a):
    for adir in sorted(glob.glob(os.path.join(LAB, "jobs", "*", "att-*"))):
        job, att = adir.split("jobs/")[1].split("/"); s = last_state(adir); ow = json.load(open(os.path.join(adir, "owner.json")))
        stage = os.path.join(LAB, "export", job, att + ".staging")
        if s and s["state"] == "terminal" and not os.path.exists(stage): continue
        alive = proc_start(ow["runner_pid"]) == ow["runner_start"]
        owned = owned_containers(att, ow["token"]); S = lambda st, **kw: append(os.path.join(adir, "state.jsonl"), dict(ts=now(), job=job, attempt=att, state=st, reconciler=True, **kw))
        if alive: print(job, att, "runner ALIVE (pid %d) -> leave alone; state=%s" % (ow["runner_pid"], s["state"])); continue
        if s["state"] == "terminal" and os.path.exists(stage):   # died mid-export after terminal? finish export from retained staging
            print(job, att, "staged export retained -> completing"); export(job, att, adir); continue
        print(job, att, f"runner DEAD; last={s['state']}; owned containers still present: {owned}")
        for c in owned: sh(["docker", "kill", c]); sh(["docker", "rm", "-f", c])
        left = owned_containers(att, ow["token"])
        if os.path.exists(os.path.join(adir, "result.json")):   # result was already produced: NEVER overwrite; just finish durable export
            print(job, att, "result.json already exists -> resuming export only (no rescoring, no overwrite)")
            S("exporting", resumed=True); export(job, att, adir)
            S("terminal", execution="completed_before_runner_death", result_sha256=sha(os.path.join(adir, "result.json")), killed=owned); continue
        partial = sorted(f for f in os.listdir(adir) if f.endswith((".stdout", ".stderr")))
        res = dict(job=job, attempt=att, execution=dict(status="interrupted"), quality=dict(assessed=False, note="incomplete data is never scored"),
                   evidence=dict(partial_files=partial), cleanup=dict(owned_containers_left=left, confirmed=not left))
        json.dump(res, open(os.path.join(adir, "result.json"), "w"), indent=1); S("exporting")
        if os.environ.get("LAB_FAULT") != "die_in_export": export(job, att, adir)
        S("terminal", execution="interrupted", result_sha256=sha(os.path.join(adir, "result.json")), killed=owned)

def exclusive_state(post):
    return None if post["compute_apps"] is None else post["compute_apps"] == []

def sampler_signal(signum, frame):
    for sampler in list(_samplers): sampler.close()
    raise SystemExit(128 + signum)

_samplers = []
def record_sampler(sampler, owner_path):
    _samplers.append(sampler)
    if sampler.reader:
        child = sampler.reader.p
        owner = json.load(open(owner_path))
        owner.update(tegrastats_pid=child.pid, tegrastats_start=proc_start(child.pid))
        json.dump(owner, open(owner_path, "w"))
    for sig in (signal.SIGTERM, signal.SIGINT, signal.SIGHUP):
        signal.signal(sig, sampler_signal)

def cleanup_sampler(owner):
    # SIGKILL cannot run handlers and can orphan tegrastats. Reconcile kills
    # only the recorded lab child, checking start time to avoid PID reuse.
    pid, start = owner.get("tegrastats_pid"), owner.get("tegrastats_start")
    if pid and start and proc_start(pid) == start:
        try: os.kill(pid, signal.SIGKILL)
        except ProcessLookupError: pass

@pytest.mark.parametrize('text,value', [('16gb', 16*1024**3), ('512mb', 512*1024**2),
                                      ('2kb', 2048), ('2b', 2), ('16g', 16*1024**3)])
def test_docker_memory_suffixes(lab, text, value):
    assert lab.memory_bytes(text) == value


def test_legacy_run_skips_admission(lab, monkeypatch, tmp_path):
    def forbidden(*args): pytest.fail('legacy admission was re-evaluated')
    monkeypatch.setattr(lab, 'admission', forbidden)
    assert lab.check_run_admission({}, str(tmp_path/'absent'), {}, {}, {}, '') == (None, 'legacy-unrecorded')


def test_admission_exception_and_deduplication(lab, admission_case):
    img, spec = admission_case
    lab.TESTS['smoke'] = {'memory': 'invalid'}
    result = lab.admission(img, spec, profile(lab), 'digest')
    assert result['decision'] == 'needs_information'
    assert 'invalid memory' in result['reasons'][0]
    lab.TESTS['smoke'] = {}
    img['platform'] = img['target_requirements']['platform'] = 'wrong'
    result = lab.admission(img, spec, profile(lab), 'digest')
    assert result['reasons'].count('platform mismatch: wrong') == 1


def test_submit_unknown_and_exception_are_complete(lab, monkeypatch, admission_case, capsys):
    img, spec = admission_case
    monkeypatch.setattr(lab, 'load_host', lambda: (profile(lab), 'digest'))
    args = SimpleNamespace(image='missing', model=spec['model'], model_sha256=None,
                           data=None, tests='smoke', key='unknown', res=None)
    lab.cmd_submit(args)
    jid = capsys.readouterr().out.strip()
    directory = Path(lab.LAB)/'jobs'/jid
    decision = json.loads((directory/'admission.json').read_text())
    assert decision['decision'] == 'needs_information'
    assert decision['reasons'] == ['unknown image record missing']
    job = json.loads((directory/'job.json').read_text())
    assert job['admission_sha256'] == lab.sha(directory/'admission.json')
    images = Path(lab.LAB)/'images'; images.mkdir()
    (images/'broken.json').write_text(json.dumps(img))
    args.image = 'broken'; args.key = 'broken'
    lab.TESTS['smoke'] = {'memory': 'bad'}
    lab.cmd_submit(args)
    directory = Path(lab.LAB)/'jobs'/capsys.readouterr().out.strip()
    assert json.loads((directory/'admission.json').read_text())['decision'] == 'needs_information'
    assert json.loads((directory/'job.json').read_text())['admission_sha256'] == lab.sha(directory/'admission.json')


def test_admission_hash_guard(lab, monkeypatch, admission_case, tmp_path):
    img, spec = admission_case
    host = profile(lab)
    path = tmp_path/'admission.json'
    path.write_text(json.dumps({'decision': 'accepted'}))
    job = {'admission_sha256': lab.sha(path)}
    assert lab.check_run_admission(job, path, img, spec, host, 'digest') == (job['admission_sha256'], 'accepted')
    path.write_text('tampered')
    with pytest.raises(SystemExit) as ex:
        lab.check_run_admission(job, path, img, spec, host, 'digest')
    assert ex.value.code == 11
    path.unlink()
    with pytest.raises(SystemExit) as ex:
        lab.check_run_admission(job, path, img, spec, host, 'digest')
    assert ex.value.code == 11


def test_declared_image_and_immutable_platform(lab, monkeypatch, admission_case):
    monkeypatch.setattr(lab, 'sh', lambda *a, **kw: SimpleNamespace(returncode=1, stderr='missing'))
    args = SimpleNamespace(name='declared', ref='remote:tag', declared=True, platform='linux/arm64', requirements=None)
    lab.cmd_image(args)
    path = Path(lab.LAB)/'images/declared.json'
    img = json.loads(path.read_text())
    assert img['verified_locally'] is False and img['provenance'] == 'declared'
    template, spec = admission_case
    img['target_requirements'] = template['target_requirements']
    result = lab.admission(img, spec, profile(lab), 'digest')
    assert result['decision'] == 'needs_information'
    assert 'image not verified on this host' in result['reasons']
    img['platform'] = 'linux/amd64'
    assert lab.admission(img, spec, profile(lab), 'digest')['decision'] == 'rejected'
    args.platform = 'linux/amd64'
    with pytest.raises(SystemExit, match='different image id/platform'): lab.cmd_image(args)
    assert json.loads(path.read_text())['platform'] == 'linux/arm64'
    args.platform = 'linux/arm64'
    monkeypatch.setattr(lab, 'sh', lambda *a, **kw: SimpleNamespace(returncode=0, stdout='sha256:new|[]|today'))
    with pytest.raises(SystemExit, match='different image id/platform'): lab.cmd_image(args)


def test_image_load_metadata_passthrough(lab, monkeypatch):
    args = lab.ap.parse_args(['image-load', 'x', '--archive', 'file', '--sha256', 'digest',
                              '--expect-config-id', 'config', '--platform', 'linux/arm64',
                              '--requirements', 'build.json'])
    monkeypatch.setattr(lab, 'sha', lambda p: 'digest')
    monkeypatch.setattr(lab, 'archive_ids', lambda p: {'config': 'config', 'index': 'index'})
    monkeypatch.setattr(lab, 'sh', lambda *a: SimpleNamespace(returncode=0, stdout='index'))
    seen = []
    monkeypatch.setattr(lab, 'cmd_image', lambda a: seen.append((a.platform, a.requirements, a.ref)))
    lab.cmd_imageload(args)
    assert seen == [('linux/arm64', 'build.json', 'index')]


def test_baseline_waits_and_times_out_with_reason(lab, monkeypatch):
    sampler = lab.MemorySampler.__new__(lab.MemorySampler)
    sampler.backend = 'tegrastats'; sampler.reader = object()
    values = iter([(None, 'waiting'), (100, None)])
    monkeypatch.setattr(sampler, 'sample', lambda: next(values))
    sleeps = []
    monkeypatch.setattr(lab.time, 'sleep', lambda seconds: sleeps.append(seconds))
    assert sampler.baseline() == (100, None)
    assert sleeps
    clock = iter([0, 0, 2])
    monkeypatch.setattr(lab.time, 'monotonic', lambda: next(clock))
    monkeypatch.setattr(sampler, 'sample', lambda: (None, 'no sample'))
    assert sampler.baseline() == (None, 'no sample')


@pytest.mark.parametrize('apps', ['[N/A]', '[Not Supported]'])
def test_optional_nvidia_fields_remain_raw(lab, monkeypatch, apps):
    responses = iter([SimpleNamespace(returncode=0, stdout='123 MiB, [N/A], 40, [Not Supported]', stderr=''),
                      SimpleNamespace(returncode=0, stdout=apps, stderr='')])
    monkeypatch.setattr(lab, 'sh', lambda *a: next(responses))
    result = lab.gpu_state(profile(lab, 'x86-main'))
    assert result['gpu'] == '123 MiB, [N/A], 40, [Not Supported]'
    assert result['compute_apps'] == [apps]
    assert lab.exclusive_state(result) is False
    assert lab.exclusive_state({'compute_apps': None}) is None
    assert lab.exclusive_state({'compute_apps': []}) is True

    assert str(ROOT/'lab/hosts/missing-host.json') in capsys.readouterr().out


def test_sampler_owner_signal_and_reconcile_cleanup(lab, monkeypatch, tmp_path):
    calls = []
    child = SimpleNamespace(pid=123)
    sampler = SimpleNamespace(reader=SimpleNamespace(p=child), close=lambda: calls.append('close'))
    owner = tmp_path/'owner.json'; owner.write_text('{}')
    monkeypatch.setattr(lab, 'proc_start', lambda pid: 'start')
    monkeypatch.setattr(lab.signal, 'signal', lambda sig, handler: calls.append((sig, handler)))
    lab.record_sampler(sampler, owner)
    saved = json.loads(owner.read_text())
    assert saved == {'tegrastats_pid': 123, 'tegrastats_start': 'start'}
    assert len(calls) == 3
    with pytest.raises(SystemExit) as ex: lab.sampler_signal(lab.signal.SIGTERM, None)
    assert ex.value.code == 128 + lab.signal.SIGTERM and calls[-1] == 'close'
    monkeypatch.setattr(lab.os, 'kill', lambda pid, sig: calls.append((pid, sig)))
    lab.cleanup_sampler(saved)
    assert calls[-1] == (123, lab.signal.SIGKILL)
    saved['tegrastats_start'] = 'other'
    before = len(calls); lab.cleanup_sampler(saved)
    assert len(calls) == before

+def check_run_admission(job, apath, img, spec, host, profile_sha):

+def sampler_signal(signum, frame):
+    for sampler in list(_samplers): sampler.close()
+    raise SystemExit(128 + signum)

+_samplers = []
+def record_sampler(sampler, owner_path):
+    _samplers.append(sampler)
+    if sampler.reader:
+        child = sampler.reader.p
+        owner = json.load(open(owner_path))
+        owner.update(tegrastats_pid=child.pid, tegrastats_start=proc_start(child.pid))
+        json.dump(owner, open(owner_path, "w"))
+    for sig in (signal.SIGTERM, signal.SIGINT, signal.SIGHUP):
+        signal.signal(sig, sampler_signal)
+
+def cleanup_sampler(owner):
+    # SIGKILL cannot run handlers and can orphan tegrastats. Reconcile kills
+    # only the recorded lab child, checking start time to avoid PID reuse.
+    pid, start = owner.get("tegrastats_pid"), owner.get("tegrastats_start")
+    if pid and start and proc_start(pid) == start:
+        try: os.kill(pid, signal.SIGKILL)
+        except ProcessLookupError: pass

+    path = tmp_path/'admission.json'
+    path.write_text(json.dumps({'decision': 'accepted'}))
+    job = {'admission_sha256': lab.sha(path)}
+    assert lab.check_run_admission(job, path, img, spec, host, 'digest') == (job['admission_sha256'], 'accepted')
+    path.write_text('tampered')
+    with pytest.raises(SystemExit) as ex:
+        lab.check_run_admission(job, path, img, spec, host, 'digest')
+    assert ex.value.code == 11
+    path.unlink()
+    with pytest.raises(SystemExit) as ex:
+        lab.check_run_admission(job, path, img, spec, host, 'digest')
+    assert ex.value.code == 11
+
+
+def test_declared_image_and_immutable_platform(lab, monkeypatch, admission_case):
+    monkeypatch.setattr(lab, 'sh', lambda *a, **kw: SimpleNamespace(returncode=1, stderr='missing'))
+    args = SimpleNamespace(name='declared', ref='remote:tag', declared=True, platform='linux/arm64', requirements=None)
+    lab.cmd_image(args)
+    path = Path(lab.LAB)/'images/declared.json'
+    img = json.loads(path.read_text())
+    assert img['verified_locally'] is False and img['provenance'] == 'declared'
+    template, spec = admission_case
+    img['target_requirements'] = template['target_requirements']
+    result = lab.admission(img, spec, profile(lab), 'digest')
+    assert result['decision'] == 'needs_information'
+    assert 'image not verified on this host' in result['reasons']
+    img['platform'] = 'linux/amd64'
+    assert lab.admission(img, spec, profile(lab), 'digest')['decision'] == 'rejected'
+    args.platform = 'linux/amd64'
+    with pytest.raises(SystemExit, match='different image id/platform'): lab.cmd_image(args)
+    assert json.loads(path.read_text())['platform'] == 'linux/arm64'
+    args.platform = 'linux/arm64'
+    monkeypatch.setattr(lab, 'sh', lambda *a, **kw: SimpleNamespace(returncode=0, stdout='sha256:new|[]|today'))
+    with pytest.raises(SystemExit, match='different image id/platform'): lab.cmd_image(args)
+
+
+def test_image_load_metadata_passthrough(lab, monkeypatch):
+    args = lab.ap.parse_args(['image-load', 'x', '--archive', 'file', '--sha256', 'digest',
+                              '--expect-config-id', 'config', '--platform', 'linux/arm64',
+                              '--requirements', 'build.json'])
+    monkeypatch.setattr(lab, 'sha', lambda p: 'digest')
+    monkeypatch.setattr(lab, 'archive_ids', lambda p: {'config': 'config', 'index': 'index'})
+    monkeypatch.setattr(lab, 'sh', lambda *a: SimpleNamespace(returncode=0, stdout='index'))
+    seen = []
+    monkeypatch.setattr(lab, 'cmd_image', lambda a: seen.append((a.platform, a.requirements, a.ref)))
+    lab.cmd_imageload(args)
+    assert seen == [('linux/arm64', 'build.json', 'index')]
+

+def test_baseline_waits_and_times_out_with_reason(lab, monkeypatch):
+    sampler = lab.MemorySampler.__new__(lab.MemorySampler)
+    sampler.backend = 'tegrastats'; sampler.reader = object()
+    values = iter([(None, 'waiting'), (100, None)])
+    monkeypatch.setattr(sampler, 'sample', lambda: next(values))
+    sleeps = []
+    monkeypatch.setattr(lab.time, 'sleep', lambda seconds: sleeps.append(seconds))
+    assert sampler.baseline() == (100, None)
+    assert sleeps
+    clock = iter([0, 0, 2])
+    monkeypatch.setattr(lab.time, 'monotonic', lambda: next(clock))
+    monkeypatch.setattr(sampler, 'sample', lambda: (None, 'no sample'))
+    assert sampler.baseline() == (None, 'no sample')
+

+@pytest.mark.parametrize('apps', ['[N/A]', '[Not Supported]'])
+def test_optional_nvidia_fields_remain_raw(lab, monkeypatch, apps):
+    responses = iter([SimpleNamespace(returncode=0, stdout='123 MiB, [N/A], 40, [Not Supported]', stderr=''),
+                      SimpleNamespace(returncode=0, stdout=apps, stderr='')])
+    monkeypatch.setattr(lab, 'sh', lambda *a: next(responses))
+    result = lab.gpu_state(profile(lab, 'x86-main'))
+    assert result['gpu'] == '123 MiB, [N/A], 40, [Not Supported]'
+    assert result['compute_apps'] == [apps]
+    assert lab.exclusive_state(result) is False
+    assert lab.exclusive_state({'compute_apps': None}) is None
+    assert lab.exclusive_state({'compute_apps': []}) is True
+

+    with pytest.raises(SystemExit) as ex: lab.load_host('missing-host')
+    assert ex.value.code == 12
+    assert str(ROOT/'lab/hosts/missing-host.json') in capsys.readouterr().out

+def test_sampler_owner_signal_and_reconcile_cleanup(lab, monkeypatch, tmp_path):
+    calls = []
+    child = SimpleNamespace(pid=123)
+    sampler = SimpleNamespace(reader=SimpleNamespace(p=child), close=lambda: calls.append('close'))
+    owner = tmp_path/'owner.json'; owner.write_text('{}')
+    monkeypatch.setattr(lab, 'proc_start', lambda pid: 'start')
+    monkeypatch.setattr(lab.signal, 'signal', lambda sig, handler: calls.append((sig, handler)))
+    lab.record_sampler(sampler, owner)
+    saved = json.loads(owner.read_text())
+    assert saved == {'tegrastats_pid': 123, 'tegrastats_start': 'start'}
+    assert len(calls) == 3
+    with pytest.raises(SystemExit) as ex: lab.sampler_signal(lab.signal.SIGTERM, None)
+    assert ex.value.code == 128 + lab.signal.SIGTERM and calls[-1] == 'close'
+    monkeypatch.setattr(lab.os, 'kill', lambda pid, sig: calls.append((pid, sig)))
+    lab.cleanup_sampler(saved)
+    assert calls[-1] == (123, lab.signal.SIGKILL)
+    saved['tegrastats_start'] = 'other'
+    before = len(calls); lab.cleanup_sampler(saved)
+    assert len(calls) == before

def test_submission_order_and_first_queued_hash(lab, monkeypatch, admission_case, capsys):
    img, spec = admission_case
    host = profile(lab)
    monkeypatch.setattr(lab, 'load_host', lambda: (host, 'digest'))
    images = Path(lab.LAB)/'images'; images.mkdir(parents=True)
    (images/'test.json').write_text(json.dumps(img))
    jdir = Path(lab.LAB)/'jobs'/('job-' + lab.sha_s('order')[:10])
    original_admission = lab._admission
    def compute(*args):
        assert not jdir.exists(), 'admission must be computed before any job writes'
        return original_admission(*args)
    monkeypatch.setattr(lab, '_admission', compute)
    writes = []
    original_dump = lab.json.dump
    def dump(value, target, **kwargs):
        writes.append(Path(target.name).name)
        if Path(target.name).name == 'job.json':
            assert (jdir/'admission.json').is_file()
            assert value['admission_sha256'] == lab.sha(jdir/'admission.json')
        return original_dump(value, target, **kwargs)
    monkeypatch.setattr(lab.json, 'dump', dump)
    lab.cmd_submit(SimpleNamespace(image='test', model=spec['model'], model_sha256=None,
                                   data=spec['data'], tests='smoke', key='order', res=None))
    assert writes[:2] == ['admission.json', 'job.json']
    job = json.loads((jdir/'job.json').read_text())
    att, adir, token, state = lab.begin_attempt(str(jdir), job['job'], job['admission_sha256'], 'accepted')
    rows = [json.loads(line) for line in (Path(adir)/'state.jsonl').read_text().splitlines()]
    assert len(rows) == 1 and rows[0]['state'] == 'queued'
    assert rows[0]['admission_sha256'] == job['admission_sha256']


def test_reconcile_cleans_orphan_even_for_terminal_attempt(lab, monkeypatch):
    adir = Path(lab.LAB)/'jobs/job-x/att-1'; adir.mkdir(parents=True)
    owner = {'runner_pid': 100, 'runner_start': 'old', 'tegrastats_pid': 200,
             'tegrastats_start': 'child', 'token': 'token'}
    (adir/'owner.json').write_text(json.dumps(owner))
    (adir/'state.jsonl').write_text(json.dumps({'state': 'terminal'}) + '\\n')
    monkeypatch.setattr(lab, 'proc_start', lambda pid: 'child' if pid == 200 else None)
    killed = []
    monkeypatch.setattr(lab.os, 'kill', lambda pid, sig: killed.append((pid, sig)))
    lab.cmd_reconcile(SimpleNamespace())
    assert killed == [(200, lab.signal.SIGKILL)]
